严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 XBB.1.16 变异株中假设性上位突变 突变 与 iSNVs 的出现及其与免疫原性决定簇改变的关联

The emergence of putative epistatic mutations and iSNVs in SARS-CoV-2 XBB.1.16 variants linked with alteration in immunogenic determinants
作者： Mukesh Kumar Jogi, Sristy Shikha, Pushpendra Singh, Devesh Sharma, Shreyansh Shreyansh, Prem Mishra, Aiswariya Priyadarsini, Deokrishna Kumar Choudhary, Meenu Jain, Ritu Sagar
摘要中文翻译严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 XBB 变异株被提出可能朝着逃避 疫苗接种 或自然感染后免疫应答的方向进化,这可能有助于其传播能力的提升。XBB.1.16 独立出现,其原因在于刺突蛋白(刺突蛋白)中积累了两个重要的氨基酸替换:E180V 和 T478R。其假病毒感染性及逃避体液免疫的能力与 XBB.1 和 XBB.1.5 相似。2023 年 3 月,XBB.1.16 在印度已超越其他主导 XBB 变异株,表明其可能具有生长优势。本研究在两个时间点对密切相关个体的 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 基因组 中的株内单核苷酸变异(iSNV)及 突变 进行筛查:症状出现时及恢复期。在恢复期检测到 XBB.1.16 变异株中假设性上位 iSNVs(E180V、G184V、G252V、D253G 和 P521S/T)的显著富集。E180V 在部分样本中与 G252V 和 P521T 形成突变组合,该模式亦在同时期的 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 基因组 中检测到。高阶蛋白质结构预测显示,E180V、G184V 与 G252V、D253G 之间的假设性上位相互作用可能与刺突蛋白折叠及结构稳定性相关。本项基于基因组学与计算分析的研究强调了这些假设性上位相互作用在免疫逃避中的潜在作用,可能是 XBB 变异株占据优势地位的原因之一。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探究 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 XBB.1.16 变异株中假设性上位突变 突变 与 iSNVs 的出现,这些变异可能改变免疫原性决定簇,从而潜在促成免疫逃避并提升传播能力。
设计与对象
本研究聚焦于 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 XBB.1.16 变异株,该变异株因刺突蛋白(刺突蛋白)中的 E180V 和 T478R 替换而独立出现。研究对象为密切相关个体,在症状出现及恢复两个时间点筛查其 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 中的株内单核苷酸变异(iSNVs)及 突变。
核心方法
本研究采用基因组学与计算分析方法,在两个时间点对密切相关个体的 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 基因组 中的 iSNVs 及 突变 进行筛查,并通过高阶蛋白质结构预测评估这些 突变 对刺突蛋白折叠与稳定性的潜在影响。
主要结果
在恢复期,XBB.1.16 变异株中假设性上位 iSNVs(E180V、G184V、G252V、D253G 和 P521S/T)富集显著。E180V 在部分样本中与 G252V 和 P521T 形成突变组合,该模式亦在同时期的 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 基因组 中检测到。
解释与创新
作者解读认为,这些假设性上位相互作用可能与免疫逃避相关,并可能是 XBB 变异株占据优势地位的原因之一。本研究强调了此类相互作用在 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 变异株进化中的作用。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可为 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 变异株的预防与监测提供参考,特别是针对具有潜在免疫逃避能力的变异株,并可指导未来关于 疫苗 及治疗策略的研究。
局限与证据强度
本研究基于摘要级证据,可能限制分析深度。证据显示 突变 与免疫逃避存在潜在关联,但需进一步研究予以确认。

The emergence of putative epistatic mutations and iSNVs in SARS-CoV-2 XBB.1.16 variants linked with alteration in immunogenic determinants

Authors: Mukesh Kumar Jogi, Sristy Shikha, Pushpendra Singh, Devesh Sharma, Shreyansh Shreyansh, Prem Mishra, Aiswariya Priyadarsini, Deokrishna Kumar Choudhary, Meenu Jain, Ritu Sagar
Original abstract (English)
The SARS-CoV-2 XBB variants have been proposed to evolve towards immune evasion against vaccination or natural infection, which may contribute to higher transmissibility. The XBB.1.16 independently emerged due to accumulation of two important substitutions, E180V and T478R in the spike protein. Its pseudoviral infectivity and evasion of humoral immunity were similar to XBB.1 and XBB.1.5. In March 2023, XBB.1.16 had outcompeted other dominant XBB variants in India, which indicate a potential growth advantage. Here, intra-host single nucleotide variations (iSNV) and mutations were screened in SARS-CoV-2 genomes in closely related individuals at two time points: at symptoms onset, and during recovery. The prominence of putative epistatic iSNVs (E180V, G184V, G252V, D253G, and P521S/T) in XBB.1.16 variants were detected during the recovery phase. E180V exhibits mutational constellations with the G252V and P521T in a subset of samples, and this pattern was also detected in contemporary SARS-CoV-2 genomes. Higher order protein structural predictions suggested that the putative epistatic interactions among E180V, G184V, and G252V, D253G may be associated with S protein folding and structural stability. This study involving genomics and computational analyses highlights the potential role of these putative epistatic interactions in immune evasion, which may have contributed to dominance of XBB variants.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the emergence of putative epistatic mutations and iSNVs in SARS-CoV-2 XBB.1.16 variants that may alter immunogenic determinants, potentially contributing to immune evasion and higher transmissibility.
Design and population
The study focused on SARS-CoV-2 XBB.1.16 variants, which independently emerged due to substitutions E180V and T478R in the spike protein. It involved screening intra-host single nucleotide variations (iSNVs) and mutations in closely related individuals at two time points: symptoms onset and recovery.
Core methods
The study used genomics and computational analyses to screen iSNVs and mutations in SARS-CoV-2 genomes from closely related individuals at two time points. It also performed higher-order protein structural predictions to assess the potential impact of these mutations on the spike protein's folding and stability.
Main results
Putative epistatic iSNVs (E180V, G184V, G252V, D253G, and P521S/T) were prominent in XBB.1.16 variants during the recovery phase. E180V was found to exhibit mutational constellations with G252V and P521T in a subset of samples, and this pattern was also detected in contemporary SARS-CoV-2 genomes.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that these putative epistatic interactions may be associated with immune evasion, potentially contributing to the dominance of XBB variants. This study highlights the role of such interactions in the evolution of SARS-CoV-2 variants.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform surveillance and monitoring of SARS-CoV-2 variants, particularly those with potential immune evasion capabilities, and could guide future research on vaccine and therapeutic development.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on abstract-only evidence, which may limit the depth of analysis. The evidence suggests a potential link between the mutations and immune evasion, but further studies are needed to confirm these findings.

Hierarchical Management of Chronic Infectious Diabetic Foot Wounds During the COVID-19 Pandemic: A Single-Center Retrospective Observational Study.

Hierarchical Management of Chronic Infectious Diabetic Foot Wounds During the COVID-19 Pandemic: A Single-Center Retrospective Observational Study.
作者： Li J, Zhou H, Jin Q, Lu H
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
The study addressed the disruption of routine chronic disease management caused by the COVID-19 pandemic, specifically focusing on the challenges faced in providing continuous care for patients with diabetic foot wounds.
设计与对象
This single-center retrospective observational study included all consecutive patients with diabetic foot treated at a single hospital between January 23 and March 27, 2020, during the COVID-19 pandemic.
核心方法
本研究回顾性收集了指定期间接受治疗的患者的人口学特征、合并症、Wagner分级、新型冠状病毒感染(治疗)策略及临床结局等数据,数据来源于电子病历。
主要结果
研究期间共收治12例患者,与2019年同期相比,医院就诊人次显著减少(P < 0.0001)。
解释与创新
The hierarchical management strategy adopted at the institution allowed essential diabetic foot care to be maintained throughout the pandemic, with no observed COVID-19 cases in this small retrospective cohort.
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,结构化的分级管理可能有助于在传染病新型冠状病毒感染(暴发)期间维持基本慢性伤口护理,但需更广泛的验证。
局限与证据强度
本研究受限于小样本量、单中心设计及回顾性研究性质,需要更大规模的研究来评估其有效性及可推广性。

Hierarchical Management of Chronic Infectious Diabetic Foot Wounds During the COVID-19 Pandemic: A Single-Center Retrospective Observational Study.

Authors: Li J, Zhou H, Jin Q, Lu H
Original abstract (English)
Coronavirus disease 2019 (COVID-19) has placed considerable pressure on healthcare systems worldwide and has disrupted the routine management of chronic diseases. Patients with diabetic foot often require repeated outpatient visits, hospitalization, and surgical intervention, making continuous wound care particularly challenging during the pandemic. This study aimed to describe the clinical characteristics of patients with diabetic foot treated during the COVID-19 pandemic and to summarize the hierarchical management strategy adopted at our institution. This single-center retrospective observational study included all consecutive patients with diabetic foot treated at our hospital between January 23 and March 27, 2020. Patient attendance during the study period was compared with that during the corresponding period in 2019. Demographic characteristics, comorbidities, Wagner classification, treatment strategies, and clinical outcomes were collected from electronic medical records and analyzed retrospectively. A total of 12 patients were treated during the study period, representing a marked reduction in hospital attendance compared with the corresponding period in 2019 (P < 0.0001). Most patients were older adults with multiple comorbidities, and 58% had Wagner grade 4-5 lesions. Surgical treatment was performed in eight patients, and the mean hospital stay was 27.5 days ± 20.2 days. No patient developed laboratory-confirmed COVID-19 during hospitalization or follow-up. One patient died of infectious shock secondary to diabetic foot infection, and the death was unrelated to COVID-19. Patients with diabetic foot often have advanced age, diabetes, multiple comorbidities, and chronic wounds that require ongoing medical care, which may increase their vulnerability during infectious disease outbreaks. The hierarchical management strategy described in this study allowed essential diabetic foot care to be maintained throughout the COVID-19 pandemic and was associated with no observed COVID-19 cases in this small retrospective cohort. Further studies involving larger populations are needed to evaluate its effectiveness and generalizability.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the disruption of routine chronic disease management caused by the COVID-19 pandemic, specifically focusing on the challenges faced in providing continuous care for patients with diabetic foot wounds.
Design and population
This single-center retrospective observational study included all consecutive patients with diabetic foot treated at a single hospital between January 23 and March 27, 2020, during the COVID-19 pandemic.
Core methods
The study retrospectively collected demographic characteristics, comorbidities, Wagner classification, treatment strategies, and clinical outcomes from electronic medical records of patients treated during the specified period.
Main results
A total of 12 patients were treated during the study period, representing a marked reduction in hospital attendance compared with the corresponding period in 2019 (P < 0.0001).
Interpretation and novelty
The hierarchical management strategy adopted at the institution allowed essential diabetic foot care to be maintained throughout the pandemic, with no observed COVID-19 cases in this small retrospective cohort.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that structured hierarchical management may help sustain essential chronic wound care during infectious disease outbreaks, though broader validation is required.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its small sample size, single-center design, and retrospective nature, necessitating larger studies to evaluate effectiveness and generalizability.

Temporal shifts in public perceptions of influenza and its vaccine before, during, and after COVID-19 in a fragile nation.

Temporal shifts in public perceptions of influenza and its vaccine before, during, and after COVID-19 in a fragile nation.
作者： Wesam S Ahmed, Abdulsalam M Halboup, Hamada R H Al-Absi, Sayida Al-Jamei, Ahmed Al-Mohamadi, Arwa Alshargabi, Yousf K Al-Ashbat, Karem H Alzoubi
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
Since the emergence of the COVID-19 pandemic, co-infection with COVID-19 and influenza has been associated with adverse health and economic outcomes. Emerging evidence suggests that the COVID-19 pandemic has influenced public perception of infections, including influenza, and acceptance of its vaccine.
设计与对象
在也门向公众分发调查问卷,共1,856名参与者完成调查(第一阶段=433人,第二阶段=981人,第三阶段=442人)。
核心方法
逻辑回归和机器学习模型突出了各阶段参与者中高流感认知及未来疫苗接种接受度的预测因子。
主要结果
与第一阶段相比,第二阶段和第三阶段的流感认知总体上有所提升。这种上升与参与者获取流感相关信息的来源变化同步。尽管认知有所提升,但未来流感疫苗接种接受度出现下降。然而,第二阶段和第三阶段的参与者在政府和医生推荐、确认疫苗安全有效且由当局免费提供的情况下,更愿意接种流感疫苗。
解释与创新
研究结果展示了脆弱国家在疫情时间线上疫苗接种行为的动态变化,并为卫生当局在设计有效的疫苗接种宣传活动及实施针对疫情和健康危机特殊挑战的疫苗接种政策方面提供可操作的见解。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为卫生当局在设计有效的疫苗接种宣传活动及实施针对疫情和健康危机特殊挑战的疫苗接种政策方面提供可操作的见解。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据未报告该研究的局限性。

Temporal shifts in public perceptions of influenza and its vaccine before, during, and after COVID-19 in a fragile nation.

Authors: Wesam S Ahmed, Abdulsalam M Halboup, Hamada R H Al-Absi, Sayida Al-Jamei, Ahmed Al-Mohamadi, Arwa Alshargabi, Yousf K Al-Ashbat, Karem H Alzoubi
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Since the emergence of the COVID-19 pandemic, co-infection with COVID-19 and influenza has been associated with adverse health and economic outcomes. Emerging evidence suggests that the COVID-19 pandemic has influenced public perception of infections, including influenza, and acceptance of its vaccine. OBJECTIVES: To assess temporal shifts in public knowledge of influenza and attitudes toward its vaccine before (Phase-I), during (Phase-II), and after (Phase-III) the COVID-19 pandemic in Yemen, a low-income, fragile nation. METHODS: A survey questionnaire was distributed to the public in Yemen, and a total of 1,856 participants completed the survey (Phase-I = 433, Phase-II = 981, Phase-III = 442). RESULTS: There was an overall increase in influenza awareness in Phases-II and III compared to Phase-I. This rise was paralleled by a change in the source from which participants gathered information about influenza. Although awareness increased, a decline in future influenza vaccine acceptance was observed. However, participants in Phases II and III showed greater willingness to receive the vaccine if it was endorsed by the government and doctors, confirmed to be safe and effective, and provided at no cost by the authorities. Logistic regression and machine-learning models highlighted predictors of high influenza awareness and future vaccine acceptance among participants in each phase. CONCLUSION: The findings illustrate vaccine behaviour dynamics in a fragile country across the pandemic timeline and provide actionable insights for health authorities for designing effective vaccination campaigns and implementing vaccination policies tailored to the unique challenges faced during pandemics and health crises.
View research seven-element analysis

Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations of the study.

COVIDTests.gov 测试套件与扩大 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 测试受试者的关联

COVIDTests.gov Test Kits Associated With Expanded SARS-CoV-2 Testing Among Recipients.
作者： Jenna Sanborn, McKaylee Robertson, Denis Nash, Kate Penrose, Avantika Srivastava, Angela M. Parcesepe, Zachary Shahn
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估通过免费直接寄送至家庭的 COVIDTests.gov 项目接收 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 检测试剂盒是否能提高美国成年人的检测率。这对于在疫情期间确保诊断检测的广泛且公平获取这一公共卫生挑战具有重要意义。
设计与对象
本研究采用差分法设计,使用来自 4549 名美国成年人的全国性 队列研究 数据。研究重点关注 COVIDTests.gov 项目(该项目于 2022 年 1 月启动)的检测试剂盒接收对 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 检测行为的影响。
核心方法
本研究采用差分法设计估计对受试者的平均 治疗 效应。研究通过分析全国性 队列研究 中 4549 名美国成年人的数据,评估与接收 COVIDTests.gov 项目免费直接寄送至家庭的检测试剂盒相关的检测率变化。
主要结果
接收该项目的检测试剂盒与 2022 年 3 月 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 检测率增加 13.8 个百分点相关(95% 置信区间为 3.3 至 24.2)。然而,后续月份的效应有所减弱。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解释为该项目在初期阶段有助于提高检测率的证据,并强调此类举措在扩大疫情期间诊断检测获取方面的潜在作用。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,有针对性地分发免费检测试剂盒可在提高检测率方面发挥作用,这对旨在改善 监测 及早期发现 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 感染的公共卫生策略具有启示意义。
局限与证据强度
本研究结果受限于潜在的未测量混杂因素及效应随时间减弱的可能性。由于依赖观察性数据且缺乏随机化,证据强度为中等。

COVIDTests.gov Test Kits Associated With Expanded SARS-CoV-2 Testing Among Recipients.

Authors: Jenna Sanborn, McKaylee Robertson, Denis Nash, Kate Penrose, Avantika Srivastava, Angela M. Parcesepe, Zachary Shahn
Original abstract (English)
Using data from 4549 US adults in a national cohort and a difference-in-differences design, we estimated the average treatment effect on the treated to assess whether receiving tests from COVIDTests.gov-a free, direct-to-household program launched in January 2022-increased testing for severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2. Receiving tests was associated with a 13.8-percentage-point testing increase in March 2022 (95% confidence interval = 3.3, 24.2), the only estimate robust to pretrend sensitivity adjustments, with later effects attenuating. Participation was lower among groups with greater health risks and access barriers. ( Am J Public Health . Published online ahead of print August 20, 2026:e1-e5. https://doi.org/10.2105/AJPH.2026.308527).
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess whether receiving SARS-CoV-2 test kits through the free, direct-to-household program COVIDTests.gov increased testing rates among US adults. This is important due to the ongoing public health challenge of ensuring widespread and equitable access to diagnostic testing during the pandemic.
Design and population
The study used a difference-in-differences design with data from 4549 US adults in a national cohort. It focused on the impact of receiving tests from the COVIDTests.gov program, which was launched in January 2022, on SARS-CoV-2 testing behavior.
Core methods
The study employed a difference-in-differences approach to estimate the average treatment effect on the treated. It analyzed data from a national cohort of 4549 US adults to assess changes in testing rates associated with receiving test kits from the free, direct-to-household program.
Main results
Receiving tests from the program was associated with a 13.8-percentage-point increase in SARS-CoV-2 testing in March 2022, with a 95% confidence interval of 3.3 to 24.2. However, the effects were observed to attenuate in later months.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as evidence that the program contributed to increased testing during its initial phase, highlighting the potential role of such initiatives in expanding access to diagnostic testing during the pandemic.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that targeted distribution of free test kits can play a role in increasing testing rates, which has implications for public health strategies aimed at improving surveillance and early detection of SARS-CoV-2 infections.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's results are limited by the potential for unmeasured confounding factors and the attenuation of effects over time. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the reliance on observational data and the lack of randomization.

变性剂与dNTP试剂通过单重与多重RT-qPCR提升严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2检测效果

Denaturing and dNTPs reagents improve SARS-CoV-2 detection via single and multiplex RT-qPCR
作者： Cristian E. Cadena-Caballero, Lina M. Vera-Cala, Carlos Barrios-Hernandez, Diego Rueda-Plata, Lizeth J. Forero-Buitrago, Torres-Jimenez CS, Erika Lizarazo-Gutierrez, Agudelo-Rodriguez M, Martinez-Perez F, Carolina S. Torres-Jiménez
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在通过RT-qPCR提升严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2检测效果,重点解决由病毒突变及靶区域RNA二级结构导致的假阴性问题。
设计与对象
研究评估了单重与多重RT-qPCR检测在49份临床样本中的性能,其中22份样本在评估的基因靶标中检测为阳性。分析样本包括符合严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2感染临床诊断的患者样本。
核心方法
研究者汇编来自GenBank与GISAID的严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2基因组序列以生成共识序列用于引物与探针设计。评估变性溶液并基于病毒核苷酸组成调整dNTP比例。收集鼻咽拭子样本并使用MagMAX试剂盒提取总RNA。
主要结果
研究使用2020年1月至12月收集的126,576条严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 基因组构建参考数据集。评估的配方在单重与多重RT-qPCR检测中与Ct值变化相关,具体取决于条件与样本集。
解释与创新
作者解读称,在评估的实验条件下,变性溶液的加入与核苷酸比例的调整影响了RT-qPCR性能。这表明RNA二级结构与核苷酸组成可能影响检测行为。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可为开发更准确且可扩展的严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2诊断工具提供参考,尤其是在病毒突变或RNA二级结构影响检测的情境下。
局限与证据强度
本研究受限于基于Ct值的性能指标及采样期间检测模式的潜在变异。由于分析的探索性质,证据强度为中等。

Denaturing and dNTPs reagents improve SARS-CoV-2 detection via single and multiplex RT-qPCR

Authors: Cristian E. Cadena-Caballero, Lina M. Vera-Cala, Carlos Barrios-Hernandez, Diego Rueda-Plata, Lizeth J. Forero-Buitrago, Torres-Jimenez CS, Erika Lizarazo-Gutierrez, Agudelo-Rodriguez M, Martinez-Perez F, Carolina S. Torres-Jiménez
Original abstract (English)
Background The COVID-19 pandemic, caused by SARS-CoV-2, highlighted the need for accurate and scalable diagnostic tools such as RT-qPCR. However, false-negative results may occur due to viral mutations and RNA secondary structures within target regions. Methods High-performance computing (HPC) was used to compile SARS-CoV-2 genomic sequences from GenBank and GISAID and generate consensus sequences for primer and probe design. A region within the ORF8 gene was selected and evaluated alongside targets from the E and N genes and the RNase P control. Nasopharyngeal swab samples were collected from patients with a prior clinical diagnosis consistent with SARS-CoV-2 infection, as well as from volunteers, and total RNA was extracted using the MagMAX kit. RT-qPCR assays were performed in both single and multiplex formats. Denaturing solutions composed of tetraethylammonium chloride (TEA) and dimethyl sulfoxide (DMSO), as well as adjusted dNTP proportions based on viral nucleotide composition, were evaluated. Exploratory Ct-based performance metrics were estimated using predefined threshold criteria with the Caret package in R. Results A total of 126,576 SARS-CoV-2 genomes collected between January and December 2020 were used to construct a reference dataset. A target region within the ORF8 gene exhibiting predicted secondary structures was selected for primer and probe design. Forty-nine clinical samples were analyzed, of which 22 tested positive across the evaluated gene targets. Variability in detection patterns was observed across sampling periods. The evaluated formulations were associated with changes in Ct values in both single and multiplex RT-qPCR assays, depending on the conditions and sample set. Conclusions The incorporation of denaturing solutions and the adjustment of nucleotide proportions were associated with changes in RT-qPCR performance under the evaluated experimental conditions. These findings suggest that RNA secondary structure and nucleotide composition may influence assay behavior; however, further studies are required to assess the broader applicability of this approach.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to improve the detection of SARS-CoV-2 using RT-qPCR by addressing challenges such as false-negative results caused by viral mutations and RNA secondary structures within target regions.
Design and population
The study evaluated the performance of single and multiplex RT-qPCR assays using 49 clinical samples, of which 22 tested positive across the evaluated gene targets. The analysis included samples collected from patients with a prior clinical diagnosis consistent with SARS-CoV-2 infection.
Core methods
The researchers compiled SARS-CoV-2 genomic sequences from GenBank and GISAID to generate consensus sequences for primer and probe design. They also evaluated denaturing solutions and adjusted dNTP proportions based on viral nucleotide composition. Nasopharyngeal swab samples were collected and total RNA was extracted using the MagMAX kit.
Main results
The study used a reference dataset of 126,576 SARS-CoV-2 genomes collected between January and December 2020. The evaluated formulations were associated with changes in Ct values in both single and multiplex RT-qPCR assays, depending on the conditions and sample set.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that the incorporation of denaturing solutions and the adjustment of nucleotide proportions influenced RT-qPCR performance under the evaluated experimental conditions. This suggests that RNA secondary structure and nucleotide composition may impact assay behavior.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform the development of more accurate and scalable diagnostic tools for SARS-CoV-2, particularly in scenarios where viral mutations or RNA secondary structures affect detection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its use of Ct-based performance metrics and the potential variability in detection patterns across sampling periods. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the exploratory nature of the analysis.

Genomic Characterisation of ST233 Pseudomonas aeruginosa Co-producing KPC-2 and VIM-2 in Northeastern Brazil During the COVID-19 Pandemic: Evidence of Independent Horizontal Acquisition Events.

Genomic Characterisation of ST233 Pseudomonas aeruginosa Co-producing KPC-2 and VIM-2 in Northeastern Brazil During the COVID-19 Pandemic: Evidence of Independent Horizontal Acquisition Events.
作者： da Silva JC, Santos ICO, da Conceição-Neto OC, Silveira MC, Sued-Karam BR, Rodrigues DCS, da Silva GA, Dimas SM, Gonzalez IHL, Pribul BR
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
共产两种碳青霉烯酶的铜绿假单胞菌给全球治疗和流行病学带来重大挑战,但巴西地区KPC和VIM共产的系统性数据仍有限。
设计与对象
2019-2023年间,通过多重PCR筛查1,489株多重耐药铜绿假单胞菌中的碳青霉烯酶编码基因,共产菌株随后进行脉冲场凝胶电泳、全基因组测序、抗菌药物敏感性试验及质粒特征分析。
核心方法
共产菌株随后进行脉冲场凝胶电泳进行克隆分型,并通过全基因组测序进行高分辨率系统发育基因组分析。同时开展抗菌药物敏感性试验及使用新一代测序平台进行质粒特征分析。
主要结果
42株菌株(2.8%)同时携带blaKPC-2和blaVIM-2,检出时间均集中在2020-2023年。PFGE鉴定出8个不同的克隆群,而WGS确认所有分离株均属于高风险ST233分型。所有共产菌株均表现出广泛耐药性,体外仅对头孢他啶-阿维巴坦和多粘菌素保持敏感。
解释与创新
分歧的基因组结构提示存在活跃的水平传播而非克隆扩散,凸显加强基因组监测与感染控制策略的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究表明,ST233分型KPC和VIM共产菌株代表了一种高风险耐药表型,具有流行病学意义,亟需加强基因组监测与感染控制措施。
局限与证据强度
本研究基于单中心数据集,且缺乏临床结局数据,限制了结果的普遍性,并限制了对新型冠状病毒感染疫情期间传播动态的因果推断。

Genomic Characterisation of ST233 Pseudomonas aeruginosa Co-producing KPC-2 and VIM-2 in Northeastern Brazil During the COVID-19 Pandemic: Evidence of Independent Horizontal Acquisition Events.

Authors: da Silva JC, Santos ICO, da Conceição-Neto OC, Silveira MC, Sued-Karam BR, Rodrigues DCS, da Silva GA, Dimas SM, Gonzalez IHL, Pribul BR
Original abstract (English)
Background Dual-carbapenemase-producing Pseudomonas aeruginosa poses a major therapeutic and epidemiological challenge worldwide, yet systematic data on KPC and VIM co-production in Brazil remain limited. The COVID-19 pandemic intensified antimicrobial use, a period temporally associated with increased carbapenemase detection globally. Objectives To characterise the molecular epidemiology and resistance profiles of KPC and VIM co-producing P. aeruginosa isolates from Brazil (2019-2023). Methods Between 2019 and 2023, 1,489 multidrug-resistant P. aeruginosa isolates were screened by multiplex PCR for carbapenemase-encoding genes. Co-producing isolates underwent pulsed-field gel electrophoresis (PFGE) for clonal profiling, followed by whole-genome sequencing (WGS) for high-resolution phylogenomic analysis. Antimicrobial susceptibility testing and plasmid characterisation using next-generation sequencing platforms were also performed. Results Forty-two isolates (2.8%) harboured both bla KPC-2 and bla VIM-2 , with detection occurring exclusively between 2020 and 2023, temporally coinciding with the COVID-19 pandemic. PFGE identified eight distinct clonal groups, providing evidence for independent horizontal gene transfer (HGT) events, whilst WGS confirmed all isolates as the high-risk ST233 lineage. Chromosomally integrated bla VIM-2 within class 1 integrons predominated; two isolates carried dual chromosomal copies. Plasmid-borne bla KPC-2 was identified across heterogeneous replicons (43.3-430.1 kb), suggesting multiple independent acquisition events. All co-producing isolates displayed extensive drug resistance, retaining in vitro susceptibility only to cefiderocol and colistin. Conclusions ST233 co-producing KPC and VIM, represents a high-risk resistance phenotype of epidemiological significance. Divergent genomic architectures suggest active horizontal dissemination across diverse genetic backgrounds rather than clonal expansion, highlighting the need for enhanced surveillance and infection control strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dual-carbapenemase-producing Pseudomonas aeruginosa poses a major therapeutic and epidemiological challenge worldwide, yet systematic data on KPC and VIM co-production in Brazil remain limited.
Design and population
Between 2019 and 2023, 1,489 multidrug-resistant Pseudomonas aeruginosa isolates were screened by multiplex PCR for carbapenemase-encoding genes, with co-producing isolates undergoing pulsed-field gel electrophoresis, whole-genome sequencing, antimicrobial susceptibility testing, and plasmid characterisation.
Core methods
Co-producing isolates underwent pulsed-field gel electrophoresis for clonal profiling, followed by whole-genome sequencing for high-resolution phylogenomic analysis. Antimicrobial susceptibility testing and plasmid characterisation using next-generation sequencing platforms were also performed.
Main results
Forty-two isolates (2.8%) harboured both bla KPC-2 and bla VIM-2, with detection occurring exclusively between 2020 and 2023. PFGE identified eight distinct clonal groups, while WGS confirmed all isolates as the high-risk ST233 lineage. All co-producing isolates displayed extensive drug resistance, retaining in vitro susceptibility only to cefiderocol and colistin.
Interpretation and novelty
Divergent genomic architectures suggest active horizontal dissemination across diverse genetic backgrounds rather than clonal expansion, highlighting the need for enhanced surveillance and infection control strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings indicate that ST233 co-producing KPC and VIM represents a high-risk resistance phenotype of epidemiological significance, warranting strengthened surveillance and infection control measures.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on a single-centre dataset and the absence of clinical outcome data, which constrains generalisability and limits causal inference regarding transmission dynamics.

The multifaceted impact of the COVID-19 pandemic on colorectal cancer care: a systematic review of management disruptions, stage migration, and survival outcomes

The multifaceted impact of the COVID-19 pandemic on colorectal cancer care: a systematic review of management disruptions, stage migration, and survival outcomes
作者： Khaled Bonna, Abdulrahman Alshawaf, Faisal Abusharkh, Meshari Alenazi, Jamil Aljehany, Afaf Bazerbashi, Maein Mare Mohammed Alshaykhi, Dalah Ajam, Genin Hasanoğlu
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
The study aimed to assess the impact of the COVID-19 pandemic on the management strategies, treatment outcomes, and survival rates of patients with colorectal cancer. The pandemic caused a catastrophic disruption to global healthcare systems, threatening timely diagnosis and treatment for colorectal cancer.
设计与对象
本系统综述遵循PRISMA指南进行,关注成年结直肠癌患者,并报告因大流行导致的治疗路径或结果的变化。综述纳入了2020年以来发表在PubMed上的研究。
核心方法
提取并综合了有关诊断延误、治疗修改、就诊时的分期以及生存率的数据。对2020年以来发表在PubMed上的研究进行了全面检索。
主要结果
纳入研究的结果综合显示了一系列负面后果。全球结直肠癌筛查严重减少,参与率下降了30-80%,结肠镜检查量下降高达92%,导致“漏诊”病例的积聚。这导致了显著的分期转移,就诊时更多患者处于晚期(如IV期)或局部晚期肿瘤状态。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为对结直肠癌治疗各个方面产生了不利影响,从筛查和诊断到治疗和结果,导致就诊时疾病分期更晚,生存率下降。这些发现突显了建立具有韧性的癌症治疗系统的重要性,以确保在未来的公共卫生危机中能够维持基本服务。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为建立具有韧性的癌症治疗系统提供了信息,以确保在未来的公共卫生危机中能够维持基本服务。它们还强调了应对大流行加剧的健康不平等和心理社会负担的重要性。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性在于其依赖于纳入研究的叙述性综合结果,这可能引入偏倚。证据强度为中等,因为这些发现基于观察性数据而非受控实验。

The multifaceted impact of the COVID-19 pandemic on colorectal cancer care: a systematic review of management disruptions, stage migration, and survival outcomes

Authors: Khaled Bonna, Abdulrahman Alshawaf, Faisal Abusharkh, Meshari Alenazi, Jamil Aljehany, Afaf Bazerbashi, Maein Mare Mohammed Alshaykhi, Dalah Ajam, Genin Hasanoğlu
Original abstract (English)
Background The COVID-19 pandemic caused a catastrophic disruption to global healthcare systems. For colorectal cancer (CRC), the world’s third most prevalent cancer, the suspension of screening programs and reallocation of resources threatened to severely impact timely diagnosis and treatment, with pre-existing models predicting significant excess mortality from even short delays. Objective This systematic review aimed to assess the impact of the COVID-19 pandemic on the management strategies, treatment outcomes, and survival rates of patients with colorectal cancer. Methods A systematic review was conducted following PRISMA guidelines. A comprehensive search of PubMed was performed for studies published from 2020 onwards. Eligible studies included those focusing on adult CRC patients and reporting on changes in care pathways or outcomes due to the pandemic. Data on diagnostic delays, treatment modifications, stage at presentation, and survival were extracted and synthesized narratively. Results The synthesis of results from the included studies revealed a cascade of negative consequences. There was a severe global reduction in CRC screening, with participation declining by 30-80% and colonoscopy volumes falling by up to 92%, leading to a pool of “missing” diagnoses. This resulted in a significant stage shift, with a higher proportion of patients presenting with advanced stage (e.g., Stage IV) and locally advanced tumors. Treatment protocols were modified, including reduced elective surgical volumes and increased use of neoadjuvant therapy. Consequently, patients diagnosed during the pandemic faced a significantly higher mortality risk, a trend exacerbated by COVID-19 co-infection, which was associated with dramatically worse survival. The pandemic also exacerbated health disparities and imposed a profound psychosocial burden on patients. Conclusion The COVID-19 pandemic had a detrimental impact on every facet of colorectal cancer care, from screening and diagnosis to treatment and outcomes, leading to more advanced disease at presentation and worsening survival. These findings underscore the critical need to develop resilient cancer care systems that can maintain essential services during future public health crises. Systematic review registration https://www.crd.york.ac.uk/PROSPERO/ , identifier CRD42025638100.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess the impact of the COVID-19 pandemic on the management strategies, treatment outcomes, and survival rates of patients with colorectal cancer. The pandemic caused a catastrophic disruption to global healthcare systems, threatening timely diagnosis and treatment for colorectal cancer.
Design and population
A systematic review was conducted following PRISMA guidelines, focusing on adult colorectal cancer patients and reporting on changes in care pathways or outcomes due to the pandemic. The review included studies published from 2020 onwards, with a comprehensive search of PubMed.
Core methods
Data on diagnostic delays, treatment modifications, stage at presentation, and survival were extracted and synthesized narratively. A comprehensive search of PubMed was performed for studies published from 2020 onwards.
Main results
The synthesis of results from the included studies revealed a cascade of negative consequences. There was a severe global reduction in CRC screening, with participation declining by 30-80% and colonoscopy volumes falling by up to 92%, leading to a pool of “missing” diagnoses. This resulted in a significant stage shift, with a higher proportion of patients presenting with advanced stage (e.g., Stage IV) and locally advanced tumors.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as a detrimental impact on every facet of colorectal cancer care, from screening and diagnosis to treatment and outcomes, leading to more advanced disease at presentation and worsening survival. These findings highlight the critical need to develop resilient cancer care systems that can maintain essential services during future public health crises.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the development of resilient cancer care systems that can maintain essential services during future public health crises. They also emphasize the importance of addressing health disparities and psychosocial burdens exacerbated by the pandemic.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on narrative synthesis of results from included studies, which may introduce bias. The evidence strength is moderate, as the findings are based on observational data rather than controlled experiments.

非住院病例中严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2感染所致上呼吸道真菌群落变化

SARS‐CoV‐2 Associated Shifts in the Upper Respiratory Tract Mycobiome in Non-hospitalized Cases
作者： Siddharth Singh Tomar, Krishna Khairnar
摘要中文翻译新型冠状病毒感染与上呼吸道真菌群落的显著变化相关。然而,非住院患者的上呼吸道真菌群落变化仍鲜有定义。我们对来自印度中部的48名有症状新型冠状病毒感染阳性个体和47名健康对照者的95份上呼吸道拭子样本进行了鸟枪法宏基因组测序。通过α多样性和β多样性分析比较了真菌多样性和群落结构,并使用基于患病率的检验和方向显著性评分(DSS)识别差异分类单元。新型冠状病毒感染阳性样本的真菌α多样性显著高于对照组,香农多样性指数(p = 0.000319)和辛普森多样性指数(p = 0.017)均显著升高。β多样性分析显示,两组在Bray-Curtis距离和Jaccard距离上均存在显著分离(PERMANOVA p = 0.001),且分散效应显著(PERMDISP p = 0.001)。DSS分析显示,某些真菌分类单元与严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2组相关,包括白假丝酵母近平滑假丝酵母(Candida orthopsilosis)、糠秕马拉色菌(Malassezia furfur)、灰绿曲霉(Aspergillus glaucus)、土曲霉(Aspergillus terreus)和黑曲霉(Aspergillus niger)的富集,而马拉色菌Arunalokei种(Malassezia arunalokei)、马槟榔曲霉(Aspergillus chevalieri)和萨氏曲霉(Aspergillus sydowii)在对照组中富集。这些发现表明,新型冠状病毒感染与上呼吸道真菌群落失调及社区病例中临床相关机会性真菌的富集相关。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2感染与上呼吸道真菌群落的显著改变相关。该研究旨在对非住院病例中的这些改变进行特征描述。
设计与对象
这项研究对中印度48名有症状的严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2阳性个体和47名健康对照者的95份上呼吸道拭子样本进行了鸟枪法宏基因组测序。研究重点关注非住院患者,以探究上呼吸道真菌群落的变化。
核心方法
通过α多样性和β多样性分析比较了真菌多样性和群落结构,并使用基于患病率的检验和方向显著性评分(DSS)识别差异分类单元。

新型冠状病毒感染阳性样本的真菌α多样性显著高于对照组,香农多样性指数(p = 0.000319)和辛普森多样性指数(p = 0.017)均显著升高。β多样性分析显示,两组在Bray-Curtis距离和Jaccard距离上均存在显著分离(PERMANOVA p = 0.001),且分散效应显著(PERMDISP p = 0.001)。DSS分析显示,某些真菌分类单元与严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2组相关,包括白假丝酵母近平滑假丝酵母(Candida orthopsilosis)、糠秕马拉色菌(Malassezia furfur)、灰绿曲霉(Aspergillus glaucus)、土曲霉(Aspergillus terreus)和黑曲霉(Aspergillus niger)的富集,而马拉色菌Arunalokei种(Malassezia arunalokei)、马槟榔曲霉(Aspergillus chevalieri)和萨氏曲霉(Aspergillus sydowii)在对照组中富集。
解释与创新
这些发现表明,严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2感染与上呼吸道真菌群失调及社区病例中临床相关机会性真菌的富集相关。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为真菌菌群失调在严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2感染中的潜在作用提供了见解,并可能为未来探索病毒与呼吸道真菌病原体间相互作用的研究提供参考。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于未住院患者,可能无法代表所有严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2感染病例。证据基于宏基因组测序与统计分析,对所观察到的关联提供中等置信度。

SARS‐CoV‐2 Associated Shifts in the Upper Respiratory Tract Mycobiome in Non-hospitalized Cases

Authors: Siddharth Singh Tomar, Krishna Khairnar
Original abstract (English)
SARS‐CoV‐2 infection is associated with marked changes of the upper respiratory tract mycobiome. URT mycobiome Changes in non-hospitalized patients however, remains poorly defined. We performed shotgun metagenomic sequencing of 95 upper respiratory tract swab samples from 48 symptomatic SARS‐CoV‐2-positive individuals and 47 healthy controls from central India. Fungal diversity and community structure were compared using alpha- and beta-diversity analyses, while differential taxa were identified using prevalence-based testing and a Directional Significance Score (DSS). SARS‐CoV‐2-positive samples showed significantly higher fungal alpha diversity than controls, with increased Shannon diversity (p = 0.000319) and Simpson diversity (p = 0.017). Beta-diversity analysis showed significant separation between groups for both Bray-Curtis and Jaccard distances (PERMANOVA p = 0.001), with significant dispersion effects as well (PERMDISP p = 0.001). DSS analysis showed certain fungal taxa associated with the SARS-CoV-2 group, including enrichment of Candida orthopsilosis, Malassezia furfur, Aspergillus glaucus, Aspergillus terreus, and Aspergillus niger, while Malassezia arunalokei, Aspergillus chevalieri, and Aspergillus sydowii were enriched in controls. These findings indicate that SARS‐CoV‐2 infection is associated with URT mycobiome dysbiosis and enrichment of clinically relevant opportunistic fungi in community cases.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
SARS‐CoV‐2 infection is associated with marked changes of the upper respiratory tract mycobiome. The study aimed to characterize these changes in non-hospitalized cases.
Design and population
The study involved shotgun metagenomic sequencing of 95 upper respiratory tract swab samples from 48 symptomatic SARS‐CoV‐2-positive individuals and 47 healthy controls from central India. It focused on non-hospitalized patients to examine URT mycobiome changes.
Core methods
Fungal diversity and community structure were compared using alpha- and beta-diversity analyses, while differential taxa were identified using prevalence-based testing and a Directional Significance Score (DSS).
Main results
SARS‐CoV‐2-positive samples showed significantly higher fungal alpha diversity than controls, with increased Shannon diversity (p = 0.000319) and Simpson diversity (p = 0.017). Beta-diversity analysis showed significant separation between groups for both Bray-Curtis and Jaccard distances (PERMANOVA p = 0.001), with significant dispersion effects as well (PERMDISP p = 0.001). DSS analysis showed certain fungal taxa associated with the SARS-CoV-2 group, including enrichment of Candida orthopsilosis, Malassezia furfur, Aspergillus glaucus, Aspergillus terreus, and Aspergillus niger, while Malassezia arunalokei, Aspergillus chevalieri, and Aspergillus sydowii were enriched in controls.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings indicate that SARS‐CoV‐2 infection is associated with URT mycobiome dysbiosis and enrichment of clinically relevant opportunistic fungi in community cases.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide insights into the potential role of fungal dysbiosis in SARS‐CoV‐2 infection and may inform future studies on the interaction between viral and fungal pathogens in the respiratory tract.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to non-hospitalized patients and may not represent all SARS‐CoV‐2 infections. The evidence is based on metagenomic sequencing and statistical analyses, which provide moderate confidence in the observed associations.

C117D 氧化模拟揭示了 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 主蛋白酶的单体结构。

The C117D oxidation mimic reveals the monomeric structure of SARS-CoV-2 main protease.
作者： Sam Andress, Matthew J McLeod, Todd Holyoak
摘要中文翻译严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 主蛋白酶(Mpro)是病毒复制的关键因子,以同源二聚体形式发挥功能,二聚化对其催化活性至关重要。Mpro 含有异常高比例的半胱氨酸残基。其中,C117 与催化核心 C145 在氧化条件下可形成可逆二硫键。为探究这些残基发生不可逆氧化后的结构与功能后果,研究者通过将这些残基替换为天冬氨酸(C117D 与 C145D)构建了氧化模拟体,以模拟亚磺酸氧化状态。动力学分析显示,两种变异体均丧失催化活性,其中 C117D 的活性较野生型(WT)Mpro 至少降低 100 倍。小角 X 射线散射(SAXS)与差示扫描荧光法(DSF)证明,C117D 在溶液中呈单体且构象不稳定,而 C145D 仍保持与 WT 相似的二聚体构象。C117D 的晶体学分析揭示了第 III 结构域发生显著重排,相对于第 I、II 结构域旋转约 40°,且 N 端与 C 端区域无序,破坏了经典的二聚化界面。局部结构变化从 C117D 位点传递至活性中心,包括解旋的氧阴离子环,为观察到的失活现象提供了结构学证据。通过形成共价 C117D-GC376 复合物,可使 C117D 恢复稳定的二聚体状态。这些发现首次高分辨率解析了单体形式的全长 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 Mpro 结构,并强调了 C117 在维持二聚化与酶活性中的关键作用。此外,单体形式中独特的第 II–III 结构域界面可能为靶向 Mpro 二聚化的别构抑制剂设计提供机遇。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探究 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 主蛋白酶(Mpro)半胱氨酸残基发生不可逆氧化后的结构与功能后果。Mpro 是病毒复制的关键因子,以同源二聚体形式发挥功能。
设计与对象
研究通过将 Mpro 的半胱氨酸残基替换为天冬氨酸以构建氧化模拟体(模拟亚磺酸氧化状态),并分析这些变异体的结构与功能特性。
核心方法
采用动力学分析、小角 X 射线散射(SAXS)、差示扫描荧光法(DSF)与晶体学分析,评估氧化模拟体 Mpro 的催化活性、构象变化与结构重排。
主要结果
动力学分析显示,C117D 与 C145D 变异体均丧失催化活性,其中 C117D 的活性较野生型(WT)Mpro 至少降低 100 倍。SAXS 与 DSF 表明,C117D 在溶液中呈单体且构象不稳定,而 C145D 仍保持与 WT 相似的二聚体构象。晶体学分析揭示 C117D 的第 III 结构域相对于第 I、II 结构域旋转约 40°,且 N 端与 C 端区域无序。
解释与创新
研究首次高分辨率解析了单体形式的全长 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 Mpro 结构,并为 C117 在维持二聚化与酶活性中的关键作用提供了结构学证据。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可能为靶向 Mpro 二聚化的别构抑制剂设计提供机遇,为开发针对 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 的治疗策略提供理论依据。
局限与证据强度
本研究基于摘要证据,结论依赖于氧化模拟体的结构与功能分析,可能无法完全反映体内环境。

The C117D oxidation mimic reveals the monomeric structure of SARS-CoV-2 main protease.

Authors: Sam Andress, Matthew J McLeod, Todd Holyoak
Original abstract (English)
The SARS-CoV-2 main protease (M pro ) is essential for viral replication and functions as a homodimer, with dimerization being critical for catalytic activity. M pro contains an unusually high number of cysteine residues. Among these, C117 and the catalytic nucleophile C145 can form a reversible disulfide bond under oxidative conditions. To investigate the structural and functional consequences of irreversible oxidation of these residues, we generated oxidation mimics by substituting these residues with aspartate (C117D and C145D), to mimic the sulfinic acid oxidation state. Kinetic assays revealed that both variants are catalytically inactive, with C117D exhibiting at least 100-fold lower activity than wild-type (WT) M pro . Small-angle X-ray scattering (SAXS) and differential scanning fluorimetry (DSF) demonstrated that C117D adopts a monomeric, destabilized state in solution, whereas C145D retains a dimeric conformation similar to WT. Crystallographic analysis of C117D revealed a dramatic rearrangement of domain III, involving a ~40° rotation relative to domains I and II, and disorder in the N- and C-terminal regions, disrupting the canonical dimerization interface. Local structural changes propagated from the C117D site to the active site, including an unwound oxyanion loop that provides structural evidence for the observed inactivity. Rescue of the stable, dimeric state for C117D was achieved through formation of the covalent C117D-GC376 complex. These findings establish a high-resolution structure of monomeric full-length SARS-CoV-2 M pro and underscore a critical role of C117 in maintaining dimerization and enzymatic function. Furthermore, the unique monomeric domain II-III interface present in the monomeric form may offer opportunities for allosteric inhibitor design targeting M pro dimerization.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the structural and functional consequences of irreversible oxidation of cysteine residues in SARS-CoV-2 main protease (M pro), which is essential for viral replication and functions as a homodimer.
Design and population
The study involved generating oxidation mimics of M pro by substituting cysteine residues with aspartate to mimic the sulfinic acid oxidation state, and analyzing the structural and functional properties of these variants.
Core methods
Kinetic assays, small-angle X-ray scattering (SAXS), differential scanning fluorimetry (DSF), and crystallographic analysis were used to assess catalytic activity, conformational changes, and structural rearrangements in the oxidation mimics of M pro.
Main results
Kinetic assays showed that both C117D and C145D variants are catalytically inactive, with C117D having at least 100-fold lower activity than wild-type (WT) M pro. SAXS and DSF indicated that C117D adopts a monomeric, destabilized state, while C145D retains a dimeric conformation. Crystallographic analysis revealed a ~40° rotation of domain III and disorder in the N- and C-terminal regions in C117D.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings establish a high-resolution structure of monomeric full-length SARS-CoV-2 M pro and provide structural evidence for the critical role of C117 in maintaining dimerization and enzymatic function.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results may offer opportunities for allosteric inhibitor design targeting M pro dimerization, which could inform the development of antiviral therapies against SARS-CoV-2.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on abstract-only evidence, and the conclusions are supported by structural and functional analyses of oxidation mimics, which may not fully represent in vivo conditions.

Dissociation Between Peripheral Muscle Strength and Exercise Capacity in Symptomatic Post-COVID-19 Individuals: Implications for Musculoskeletal Rehabilitation.

Dissociation Between Peripheral Muscle Strength and Exercise Capacity in Symptomatic Post-COVID-19 Individuals: Implications for Musculoskeletal Rehabilitation.
作者： Silva KFMD, Felipe Sczepanski, Vagner Pires de Campos Júnior, Claudia Brunnquell, Kaique Fernando Macedo da Silva
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
Infection with SARS-CoV-2 may result in persistent functional impairment after the acute phase of the disease. Emerging evidence suggests that the presence and intensity of COVID-19-related symptoms may be associated with deficits in muscle strength and physical performance during the post-infection period.
设计与对象
本横断面前瞻性研究纳入 76 名年龄 18-77 岁的成人,均在巴西统一医疗系统注册。参与者基于临床记录被分类为症状个体或无症状个体。
核心方法
体力活动水平通过国际体力活动问卷(IPAQ)评估。体能表现通过握力测试评估外周肌肉力量,并通过渐进性往返步行测试(ISWT)评估运动能力。采用多元协方差分析(MANOVA)与单变量协方差分析(ANCOVA)进行统计分析,并按年龄、性别及体力活动水平进行协变量调整。
主要结果
The presence of COVID-19-related symptoms was independently associated with lower handgrip strength (p = 0.0099) after adjustment for covariates. No significant differences were observed in exercise capacity between the symptomatic and asymptomatic groups (p = 0.497).
解释与创新
Symptomatic individuals after COVID-19 exhibited reduced peripheral muscle strength despite no significant differences in exercise capacity between the groups. These findings underscore the relevance of handgrip strength assessment as a simple and sensitive tool for identifying residual functional impairment in post-COVID-19 follow-up.
科研与公卫意义
The findings highlight the importance of assessing handgrip strength as a tool for identifying residual functional impairment in post-COVID-19 individuals, which can inform targeted musculoskeletal rehabilitation strategies.
局限与证据强度
本研究受限于横断面设计及潜在混杂因素,但因采用协变量调整统计分析及明确人群,证据强度较高。

Dissociation Between Peripheral Muscle Strength and Exercise Capacity in Symptomatic Post-COVID-19 Individuals: Implications for Musculoskeletal Rehabilitation.

Authors: Silva KFMD, Felipe Sczepanski, Vagner Pires de Campos Júnior, Claudia Brunnquell, Kaique Fernando Macedo da Silva
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: Infection with SARS-CoV-2 may result in persistent functional impairment after the acute phase of the disease. Emerging evidence suggests that the presence and intensity of COVID-19-related symptoms may be associated with deficits in muscle strength and physical performance during the post-infection period. Our objective was to compare physical performance after the acute phase of COVID-19 between symptomatic and asymptomatic individuals, focusing on exercise capacity and peripheral muscle strength. METHODS: This cross-sectional, prospective study included 76 adults aged 18-77 years, registered in the Brazilian Unified Health System. Participants were classified as symptomatic or asymptomatic based on clinical records. Physical activity level was assessed using the International Physical Activity Questionnaire (IPAQ). Physical performance was evaluated through handgrip strength and exercise capacity measured by the Incremental Shuttle Walking Test (ISWT). Multivariate analysis of covariance (MANOVA) and univariate analyses (ANCOVA) were performed, adjusted for age, sex, and physical activity level. RESULTS: The presence of COVID-19-related symptoms was independently associated with lower handgrip strength (p = 0.0099) after adjustment for covariates. No significant differences were observed in exercise capacity between the symptomatic and asymptomatic groups (p = 0.497). Effect size analysis demonstrated a moderate association between symptom presence and peripheral muscle strength, whereas the association with exercise capacity was small. CONCLUSION: Symptomatic individuals after COVID-19 exhibited reduced peripheral muscle strength despite no significant differences in exercise capacity between the groups. These findings underscore the relevance of handgrip strength assessment as a simple and sensitive tool for identifying residual functional impairment in post-COVID-19 follow-up.
View research seven-element analysis

Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design and potential confounding factors, but the evidence is strong due to the use of adjusted statistical analyses and a defined population.

重组刺突蛋白基础上研发的疫苗接种在不同剂量下对严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2进化动态及变异株出现的剂量依赖性影响

Dose-dependent effects of recombinant spike protein-based vaccination on SARS-CoV-2 evolutionary dynamics and the emergence of variants in hamster models
作者： Kyuyoung Shim, Jeong‐Hwa Choi, Eun-Ha Hwang, Green Kim, Gyu‐Seo Bae, Eunsu Jeon, Seung Ho Baek, Hee‐Sung Kim, Seok‐Hwan Kim, Dae-Soo Kim
摘要中文翻译严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2(严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2)通过持续出现新型变异株在全球持续传播。疫苗接种被视为可通过施加免疫压力影响严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2进化的选择性环境之一。本研究调查了在接种不同剂量全长刺突蛋白疫苗的仓鼠体内,Delta变异株的进化动态,以反映由疫苗接种诱导的免疫水平异质性。在仓鼠模型中,较高剂量疫苗可预防肺部病毒复制,但仅部分抑制鼻腔内复制。在排除未接种对照组中检测到的株内单核苷酸变异(iSNV)后,通过调整测序深度的负二项式模型显示,鼻腔样本中刺突蛋白、ORF1a、ORF1b及ORF3a基因的iSNV发生率随疫苗接种剂量显著增加,且阳性选择信号主要见于最高剂量组。iSNV呈现多样且独特的组合,且每只个体独有,反映了株内进化的个体特异性模式。在至少两只接种个体中检测到的某些氨基酸替换在Omicron变异株中更常见。纳入个体特异性iSNV的计算机模拟分析显示,仅在某些接种个体中鉴定出的变异株对1类和3类中和抗体的结合亲和力降低。综上所述,研究结果表明,异质性疫苗接种诱导的免疫压力可在剂量依赖且株内特异的方式下塑造严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2进化动态,并凸显部分免疫及上呼吸道iSNV增加在推动潜在免疫逃逸变异株出现中的潜在作用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探究不同剂量的疫苗对严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2进化动态及新变异株出现的影响,因严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2通过持续出现新变异株在全球持续传播。
设计与对象
该研究使用仓鼠模型,检验了严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 Delta变异株在不同剂量的完整刺突蛋白 疫苗作用下的进化动态,反映了疫苗接种诱导的免疫水平异质性。
核心方法
研究人员采用负二项式模型调整测序深度,分析鼻腔样本中的株内单核苷酸变异(iSNV),并开展计算机模拟分析以评估对中和抗体的结合亲和力。
主要结果
在排除未接种疫苗对照组的iSNV后,鼻拭子样本中刺突蛋白、ORF1a、ORF1b及ORF3a基因的iSNV发生率呈现显著的疫苗剂量依赖性升高,且阳性选择信号主要集中于最高剂量组。体外分析显示,某些接种疫苗个体的变异株对1类和3类中和抗体的结合亲和力降低。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,异质性疫苗诱导的免疫压力可在剂量依赖且宿主特异性的方式下塑造严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2体内的进化进化,并凸显部分免疫状态及上呼吸道内iSNVs数量增加在驱动免疫逃逸变异株出现中的潜在作用。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果为疫苗接种策略如何影响严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2进化动态提供了见解,特别是在部分免疫及免疫逃逸变异株出现的背景下,可为未来疫苗研发及公共卫生措施提供参考。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅限于仓鼠模型,可能无法完全反映人类免疫应答;证据基于计算机模拟分析,可能存在不确定性。由于仅基于摘要证据及模型推断,对这些发现的信心为中等。

Dose-dependent effects of recombinant spike protein-based vaccination on SARS-CoV-2 evolutionary dynamics and the emergence of variants in hamster models

Authors: Kyuyoung Shim, Jeong‐Hwa Choi, Eun-Ha Hwang, Green Kim, Gyu‐Seo Bae, Eunsu Jeon, Seung Ho Baek, Hee‐Sung Kim, Seok‐Hwan Kim, Dae-Soo Kim
Original abstract (English)
Severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2 (SARS-CoV-2) has continued to circulate globally through the persistent emergence of novel variants. Vaccination has been regarded as one of the selective environments that can influence SARS-CoV-2 evolution by exerting immune pressure. This study investigated the evolutionary dynamics of the SARS-CoV-2 Delta variant in hamsters immunized with varying doses of a full-length spike protein vaccine, potentially reflecting the heterogeneous levels of immunity. In hamster models, higher vaccine doses prevented viral replication in the lungs but only partially suppressed replication in the nasal passages. After excluding intra-host single-nucleotide variants (iSNVs) detected in non-vaccinated controls, a negative binomial model adjusting for read depth revealed a significant vaccine dose-dependent increase in iSNV occurrence in the Spike, ORF1a, ORF1b and ORF3a genes in nasal samples and positive selection signals were predominantly observed in the highest vaccine dose group. The iSNVs were observed in diverse and distinct combinations that were unique to each individual, reflecting host-specific intra-host mutation patterns. Some aa substitutions detected in at least two individuals in the vaccinated group were more frequently observed in the Omicron variant. In silico analyses incorporating individual-specific iSNVs demonstrated that reduced binding affinity to class 1 and 3 neutralizing antibodies was observed exclusively in variants identified from certain vaccinated individuals. In conclusion, these findings indicate that heterogeneous vaccine-induced immune pressure can shape intra-host SARS-CoV-2 evolution in a dose-dependent and host-specific manner and highlight the potential role of partial immunity and increased iSNVs in the upper respiratory tract in driving the emergence of putative immune-evasive viral variants.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate how varying vaccine doses affect the evolutionary dynamics of SARS-CoV-2 and the emergence of variants, as SARS-CoV-2 has continued to circulate globally through the persistent emergence of novel variants.
Design and population
The study used hamster models to examine the evolutionary dynamics of the SARS-CoV-2 Delta variant in response to different doses of a full-length spike protein vaccine, reflecting the heterogeneous levels of immunity induced by vaccination.
Core methods
The researchers employed a negative binomial model to adjust for read depth and analyze intra-host single-nucleotide variants (iSNVs) in nasal samples, while also conducting in silico analyses to assess binding affinity to neutralizing antibodies.
Main results
After excluding iSNVs from non-vaccinated controls, a significant vaccine dose-dependent increase in iSNV occurrence was observed in the Spike, ORF1a, ORF1b and ORF3a genes in nasal samples, with positive selection signals predominantly in the highest dose group. In silico analyses showed reduced binding affinity to class 1 and 3 neutralizing antibodies in variants from certain vaccinated individuals.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that heterogeneous vaccine-induced immune pressure can shape intra-host SARS-CoV-2 evolution in a dose-dependent and host-specific manner, highlighting the potential role of partial immunity and increased iSNVs in the upper respiratory tract in driving the emergence of immune-evasive variants.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results provide insights into how vaccination strategies may influence SARS-CoV-2 evolution, particularly in the context of partial immunity and the emergence of immune-evasive variants, which could inform future vaccine development and public health measures.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to hamster models, which may not fully reflect human immune responses, and the evidence is based on in silico analyses, which may have uncertainties. The confidence in these findings is moderate due to the abstract-only evidence and model-based interpretations.

多抗原新型冠状病毒疫苗疫苗诱导强效且持久的交叉保护作用,对多种严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2变异株有效

Multi-antigen coronavirus vaccine induces potent and durable cross-protection against multiple SARS-CoV-2 variants
作者： Swayam Prakash, Nisha R. Dhanushkodi, Afshana Quadiri, Hawa Vahed, Aziz A. Chentoufi, Pierre-Gregoire Coulon, Izabela Coimbra Ibraim, Assia El Babsiri, Delia F. Tifrea, Cesar J. Figueroa
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在解决第一代基于刺突蛋白的新型冠状病毒疫苗疫苗的局限性,该疫苗无法阻止免疫逃逸并延长了疫情。研究试图开发一种可提供广泛且持久跨保护作用的下一代新型冠状病毒疫苗疫苗,以对抗多种变异株。
设计与对象
该研究涉及识别不同 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 毒株及其变异株间的保守抗原,并向金黄地鼠(Syrian hamster)接种三种保守抗原,以评估其对多种关注变异株的保护效果。
核心方法
研究人员在严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2毒株、先前的关注变异株及相关冠状病毒中识别出10个非刺突蛋白抗原。随后,研究人员在金黄地鼠中测试这些抗原,以评估其诱导免疫应答的能力并对不同变异株提供保护的效果。
主要结果
3个保守抗原(NSP2、NSP14、核蛋白)可诱导高频率的肺组织驻留多功能T细胞,并降低发病率、病毒载量、肺部病理损伤及症状,包括Delta与Omicron变异株。保护作用持续超过1年。
解释与创新
作者认为,多抗原新型冠状病毒疫苗疫苗可提供广谱且持久的跨保护性免疫,对抗当前及未来变异株,这是新型冠状病毒疫苗研发的新颖策略。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,该新型冠状病毒疫苗可通过提供对新兴变异株的保护,增强疫苗接种与预防策略,并可能减少新型冠状病毒疫苗配方的频繁更新需求。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅限于动物模型,且证据基于摘要数据,可能无法完全转化为人类结局。由于缺乏详细临床数据,证据强度为中等。

Multi-antigen coronavirus vaccine induces potent and durable cross-protection against multiple SARS-CoV-2 variants

Authors: Swayam Prakash, Nisha R. Dhanushkodi, Afshana Quadiri, Hawa Vahed, Aziz A. Chentoufi, Pierre-Gregoire Coulon, Izabela Coimbra Ibraim, Assia El Babsiri, Delia F. Tifrea, Cesar J. Figueroa
Original abstract (English)
Abstract The first-generation Spike-based COVID-19 vaccines reduced the risk of hospitalization, serious illness, and death from SARS-CoV-2 infections. However, waning immunity failed to prevent immune escape, resulting in multiple variants of concern (VOCs) and prolonging the pandemic. We hypothesize that next-generation CoV vaccines incorporating highly conserved SARS-CoV-2 T cell antigens would confer potent, broad, long-lasting cross-protective immunity against multiple VOCs. In this study, we identified 10 non-Spike antigens common and highly conserved across 8.7 million SARS-CoV-2 strains, prior VOCs, SARS-CoV-1, MERS-CoV, seasonal human cold CoVs, and animal CoVs. Seven antigens were preferentially recognized by CD8 + and CD4 + T cells from unvaccinated asymptomatic COVID-19 patients, irrespective of VOC. Three conserved antigens (NSP2, NSP14, Nucleoprotein) administered to golden Syrian hamsters: ( i ) induced high frequencies of lung-resident polyfunctional CXCR5 + CD4 + T FH cells, GzmB + CD4 + /CD8 + cytotoxic T cells, and CD69 + IFN-γ + TNFα + CD4 + /CD8 + effector T cells; ( ii ) reduced morbidity, viral load, lung pathology, and COVID-19-like symptoms from various VOCs, including the pathogenic B.1.617.2 Delta and the recent KP.3 Omicron variants; ( iii ) improved protection conferred by spike-alone mRNA; and ( iv ) conferred protection that lasted for more than 1 year. This multi-antigen CoV vaccine could provide potent, broad-spectrum, and durable cross-protective immunity against current and future variants of concern in the clinic.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the limitations of first-generation Spike-based vaccines, which failed to prevent immune escape and prolonged the pandemic. The research sought to develop a next-generation vaccine that could provide broad and durable cross-protection against multiple variants.
Design and population
The study involved identifying conserved antigens across various SARS-CoV-2 strains and variants, and administering three conserved antigens to golden Syrian hamsters to evaluate their protective effects against multiple variants of concern.
Core methods
The researchers identified 10 non-Spike antigens common across SARS-CoV-2 strains, prior VOCs, and related coronaviruses. They then tested these antigens in golden Syrian hamsters to assess their ability to induce immune responses and confer protection against different variants.
Main results
The three conserved antigens (NSP2, NSP14, Nucleoprotein) induced high frequencies of lung-resident polyfunctional T cells and reduced morbidity, viral load, lung pathology, and symptoms from various VOCs, including Delta and Omicron variants. Protection lasted for more than 1 year.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that the multi-antigen vaccine could provide broad-spectrum and durable cross-protective immunity against current and future variants, which is a novel approach to vaccine development.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that this vaccine could enhance surveillance and prevention strategies by offering protection against emerging variants, potentially reducing the need for frequent updates to vaccine formulations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to animal models, and the evidence is based on abstract-only data, which may not fully translate to human outcomes. The evidence strength is moderate due to the lack of detailed clinical data.

Violence, Stigma, and Moral Injury in Nursing During the COVID-19 Pandemic: A Qualitative Analysis From 18 Countries in Latin America and the Caribbean.

Violence, Stigma, and Moral Injury in Nursing During the COVID-19 Pandemic: A Qualitative Analysis From 18 Countries in Latin America and the Caribbean.
作者： Liana Amorim Corrêa Trotte, Marluci Andrade Conceição Stipp, Mesquita MGDR, Javier Isidro Rodríguez-López, Juana Jiménez-Sánchez, Sandra Milena Aponte-Franco, María Guadalupe Casales-Hernández, Gabriela Castillo-Veras, Paola Andrea Dixon, Julio Alejandro García García-Castillo
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
The study addressed the intensification of workplace violence against nurses and midwives during the COVID-19 pandemic, highlighting how structural marginalization in health systems across Latin America and the Caribbean was exposed and amplified by the crisis.
设计与对象
研究采用二次定性分析方法,基于全球联盟开发的国际在线调查问卷的文本回复,覆盖18个拉丁美洲和加勒比国家的3860名护士和助产士。
核心方法
数据通过IRaMuTeQ软件进行降序分层分类,随后通过反思性主题分析进行解释性整合,以识别叙事中的反复模式。
主要结果
分析识别出五个词汇类别,并重组为三个相互关联的主题:与护理实施和系统崩溃相关的直接暴力;污名、恐惧与社会归属感的破坏;以及与结构性指责、道德痛苦和道德伤害相关的伦理-政治性痛苦。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解释为大流行期间针对护理专业人员的暴力是一种结构性且多面的现象,根植于历史不平等与制度脆弱性,且在不同情境下呈现出情绪超负荷、专业去合法化及多重暴力形式暴露的反复模式。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调,在常规医疗环境和未来公共卫生紧急事件中,需持续推行强化劳动力保护、伦理问责与制度支持的政策。
局限与证据强度
本研究基于调查回复的二次定性分析,可能限制结果的普遍性并引入选择或回复偏倚;由于仅基于摘要且缺乏直接参与者验证,证据强度被评估为中等。

Violence, Stigma, and Moral Injury in Nursing During the COVID-19 Pandemic: A Qualitative Analysis From 18 Countries in Latin America and the Caribbean.

Authors: Liana Amorim Corrêa Trotte, Marluci Andrade Conceição Stipp, Mesquita MGDR, Javier Isidro Rodríguez-López, Juana Jiménez-Sánchez, Sandra Milena Aponte-Franco, María Guadalupe Casales-Hernández, Gabriela Castillo-Veras, Paola Andrea Dixon, Julio Alejandro García García-Castillo
Original abstract (English)
Workplace violence against nurses and midwives intensified during the COVID-19 pandemic, exposing and amplifying longstanding structural marginalization in health systems across Latin America. This study analyzes how different forms of violence were experienced and described by nursing professionals during this period. A secondary qualitative analysis was conducted using text-based responses from an international online survey developed by the Global Consortium, which included 3860 nurses and midwives from 18 Latin American and Caribbean countries. Data were examined through Descending Hierarchical Classification using IRaMuTeQ, followed by reflective thematic analysis for interpretive integration. The analysis identified five lexical classes, reorganized into three interconnected themes: direct violence related to care delivery and system collapse; stigma, fear, and disruption of social belonging; and ethical-political suffering associated with structural blame, moral suffering, and moral injury. Despite contextual differences across countries, narratives revealed recurring patterns of emotional overload, professional delegitimization, and exposure to multiple forms of violence. These findings position violence against nursing professionals during the pandemic as a structural and multifaceted phenomenon rooted in historical inequities and institutional fragility, underscoring the need for sustained policies that strengthen workforce protection, ethical accountability, and institutional support in both routine healthcare settings and future public health emergencies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the intensification of workplace violence against nurses and midwives during the COVID-19 pandemic, highlighting how structural marginalization in health systems across Latin America and the Caribbean was exposed and amplified by the crisis.
Design and population
A secondary qualitative analysis was conducted using text-based responses from an international online survey developed by the Global Consortium, which included 3860 nurses and midwives from 18 Latin American and Caribbean countries.
Core methods
Data were examined through Descending Hierarchical Classification using IRaMuTeQ, followed by reflective thematic analysis for interpretive integration to identify recurring patterns in the narratives.
Main results
The analysis identified five lexical classes, reorganized into three interconnected themes: direct violence related to care delivery and system collapse; stigma, fear, and disruption of social belonging; and ethical-political suffering associated with structural blame, moral suffering, and moral injury.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as evidence that violence against nursing professionals during the pandemic was a structural and multifaceted phenomenon rooted in historical inequities and institutional fragility, with recurring patterns of emotional overload, professional delegitimization, and exposure to multiple forms of violence across diverse contexts.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings underscore the need for sustained policies that strengthen workforce protection, ethical accountability, and institutional support in both routine healthcare settings and future public health emergencies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on secondary qualitative analysis of survey responses, which may limit generalizability and introduce selection or response bias; the evidence strength is assessed as moderate due to the abstract-only scope and lack of direct participant validation.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述A high priorityAcademic literature · Review

Convalescent Plasma Restores Viral Clearance After Anti-Spike Monoclonal Antibody Failure in Immunocompromised COVID-19 Patients: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis of Individual Patient Data.

Convalescent Plasma Restores Viral Clearance After Anti-Spike Monoclonal Antibody Failure in Immunocompromised COVID-19 Patients: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis of Individual Patient Data.
作者： Daniele Focosi, Tommaso Francesco Aiello, Arturo Casadevall, Mario Cruciani, Alessandra D'Abramo, Patrizia De Marco, David Gachoud, Carolina Garcia-Vidal, Claudia Glingani, Thomas Hueso
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
The review examines the efficacy of high-titre COVID-19 convalescent plasma (CCP) in restoring viral clearance among immunocompromised patients with persistent SARS-CoV-2 viraemia following failure of anti-Spike monoclonal antibody (mAb) therapy, focusing on the central question of whether CCP can overcome resistance to mAbs in this population.
证据基础与方法
进行了系统评价和个体参与者数据荟萃分析,检索了 2020 年 1 月至 2026 年 1 月的电子数据库。该方案在 PROSPERO 注册(CRD420251142891)。纳入了 47 例病例(12 项已发表研究中的 30 例和 17 例未发表病例),资格标准包括免疫功能低下患者在 mAb 治疗后 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 持续呈阳性。
共识与结论
该综述得出的结论是,在 70.2% (33/47) 的 mAb 耐药感染免疫功能低下患者中,CCP 输注与 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 清除相关,平均输注 2.7 个 CCP 单位。在逻辑回归分析中,CCP 输注总量与病毒清除率呈正相关。
争议与缺口
该综述没有报告长期结果、病毒清除的持久性或与较新的 抗病毒药物 药物的疗效比较。此外,样本量小(47 个案例)和对未发表案例的依赖限制了结论的普遍性和稳健性。
科研与实践启示
该综述强调需要进行随机对照试验来验证 CCP 在免疫功能低下人群中的疗效并确定最佳剂量策略。建议将 监测 用于对 mAb 和 CCP 均具有抗性的新兴 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 变体,以指导 治疗 方案。

Convalescent Plasma Restores Viral Clearance After Anti-Spike Monoclonal Antibody Failure in Immunocompromised COVID-19 Patients: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis of Individual Patient Data.

Authors: Daniele Focosi, Tommaso Francesco Aiello, Arturo Casadevall, Mario Cruciani, Alessandra D'Abramo, Patrizia De Marco, David Gachoud, Carolina Garcia-Vidal, Claudia Glingani, Thomas Hueso
Original abstract (English)
Anti-Spike monoclonal antibodies (mAbs) progressively lost efficacy during the COVID-19 pandemic due to the emergence and predominance of resistant SARS-CoV-2 variants. By contrast, high-titre COVID-19 convalescent plasma (CCP) collected from vaccinated donors recently recovered from infection provides a polyclonal source of antibodies that remains effective in clearing SARS-CoV-2 in immunosuppressed patients, unable to mount an adequate immune response against the virus. We conducted a systematic review and individual participant data meta-analysis to examine the effect of CCP in immunocompromised patients persistently positive for SARS-CoV-2 viraemia following mAb therapy, and to evaluate possible biological factors associated with a favourable outcome. Electronic databases were searched for studies published from January 2020 to January 2026. All studies including eligible cases were considered in the systematic review. Unpublished cases were also collected from investigators of the selected studies. The protocol was registered at PROSPERO (CRD420251142891). Forty-seven cases (30 cases from 12 published studies and 17 unpublished cases) were included. In 33 out of 47 patients (70.2%) with a mAb-resistant infection, SARS-CoV-2 clearance occurred following CCP transfusion, with a mean of 2.7 CCP units administered. In logistic regression, total CCP volume transfused was positively associated with SARS-CoV-2 clearance. In conclusion, CCP transfusion was associated with SARS-CoV-2 clearance in persistently positive immunocompromised patients following failure of anti-Spike mAb therapy.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines the efficacy of high-titre COVID-19 convalescent plasma (CCP) in restoring viral clearance among immunocompromised patients with persistent SARS-CoV-2 viraemia following failure of anti-Spike monoclonal antibody (mAb) therapy, focusing on the central question of whether CCP can overcome resistance to mAbs in this population.
Evidence base and method
A systematic review and individual participant data meta-analysis was conducted, searching electronic databases from January 2020 to January 2026. The protocol was registered at PROSPERO (CRD420251142891). Forty-seven cases (30 from 12 published studies and 17 unpublished cases) were included, with eligibility criteria encompassing immunocompromised patients persistently positive for SARS-CoV-2 after mAb therapy.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that CCP transfusion was associated with SARS-CoV-2 clearance in 70.2% (33/47) of immunocompromised patients with mAb-resistant infections, with a mean of 2.7 CCP units administered. Total CCP volume transfused was positively associated with viral clearance in logistic regression analysis.
Controversies and gaps
The review does not report long-term outcomes, durability of viral clearance, or comparative efficacy against newer antiviral agents. Additionally, the small sample size (47 cases) and reliance on unpublished cases limit generalizability and robustness of conclusions.
Research and practice implications
The review highlights the need for randomized controlled trials to validate CCP efficacy in immunocompromised populations and to determine optimal dosing strategies. Surveillance for emerging SARS-CoV-2 variants resistant to both mAbs and CCP is recommended to guide treatment protocols.

Timing, Titre, and Host Determinants of Convalescent Plasma Efficacy in COVID-19: A Systematic Review With Design-Stratified Narrative Synthesis.

Timing, Titre, and Host Determinants of Convalescent Plasma Efficacy in COVID-19: A Systematic Review With Design-Stratified Narrative Synthesis.
作者： Agnieszka Kuś, Sebastian Makuch, Jarosław Dybko, Siddarth Agrawal
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
The review examines the efficacy of convalescent plasma (CP) for COVID-19 caused by SARS-CoV-2, focusing on three interacting determinants: timing of administration, antibody titre, and host characteristics, to reconcile conflicting trial outcomes.
证据基础与方法
进行了设计分层叙述综合的系统评价(PRISMA 2020;SWiM),检索 PubMed、Web of Science 和 Scopus,直至 2026 年 5 月,将标准化的 91 场提取矩阵应用于 31 项纳入研究(20,793 名患者;8 项随机对照试验,23 项观察性研究)。
共识与结论
CP 仅在特定条件下有效:早期给药、足够的抗体滴度以及缺乏内源性抗体反应的血清阴性或免疫功能低下的 宿主。在随机对照试验中,6 项中的 4 项发现早期给药有益,4 项中的 3 项发现较高滴度的 CP 有益,两项大型随机对照试验 (n=12,522) 显示对血清阴性受者有优先益处。
争议与缺口
CP 产品中的非 IgG 免疫球蛋白 (IgA/IgM) 的表征几乎不存在,尽管有证据表明它们对 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 具有有效的中和活性,但 31 项研究中只有 2 项报告了此类数据,这代表了产品评估中的系统性差距。
科研与实践启示
未来的研究应优先考虑 CP 产品中 IgA/IgM 滴度的标准化量化,并评估未来大流行中被动免疫治疗的精确部署策略,利用时机和滴度决定因素来优化疗效。

Timing, Titre, and Host Determinants of Convalescent Plasma Efficacy in COVID-19: A Systematic Review With Design-Stratified Narrative Synthesis.

Authors: Agnieszka Kuś, Sebastian Makuch, Jarosław Dybko, Siddarth Agrawal
Original abstract (English)
The efficacy of convalescent plasma (CP) in COVID-19 has been contested, with large randomized trials producing conflicting results that led to recommendations against routine use. We performed a systematic review with design-stratified narrative synthesis (PRISMA 2020; SWiM; OSF: https://osf.io/tjcuq) to test whether these conflicting outcomes can be reconciled through three interacting determinants-timing, antibody titre, and host characteristics-using a standardized 91-field extraction matrix applied to each study. PubMed, Web of Science, and Scopus were searched through May 2026. Thirty-one studies (20,793 patients; 8 RCTs, 23 observational) were included. The synthesis indicates that CP is effective, but only under a specific convergence of conditions: when administered early in the disease course, with sufficient antibody titre, to seronegative or immunocompromised hosts who have not yet mounted an endogenous antibody response. Among RCTs analysing timing, 4 of 6 found benefit with earlier administration; for titre, 3 of 4 RCTs with explicit comparisons found benefit with higher-titre CP; and two large RCTs (combined n = 12,522) demonstrated preferential benefit in seronegative recipients. This interpretation is consistent with modelling estimates that CP, deployed largely to hospitalised patients, nonetheless saved tens of thousands of lives in the United States during the first pandemic year, an effect attributed to the subset treated early enough to modify disease progression. A second, previously unquantified finding concerns antibody class: characterisation of non-IgG immunoglobulins was nearly absent, with only 2 of 31 studies-both from our group-reporting IgA or IgM data, despite evidence that IgA and IgM are among the most potent neutralising classes against SARS-CoV-2. This hypothesis-generating observation identifies a systematic gap in CP product assessment. These findings reframe CP efficacy from a binary question to a conditional one and provide a template for the precision deployment of passive immunotherapy in future pandemics.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines the efficacy of convalescent plasma (CP) for COVID-19 caused by SARS-CoV-2, focusing on three interacting determinants: timing of administration, antibody titre, and host characteristics, to reconcile conflicting trial outcomes.
Evidence base and method
A systematic review with design-stratified narrative synthesis (PRISMA 2020; SWiM) was conducted, searching PubMed, Web of Science, and Scopus through May 2026, applying a standardized 91-field extraction matrix to 31 included studies (20,793 patients; 8 RCTs, 23 observational).
Consensus and conclusions
CP is effective only under specific conditions: early administration, sufficient antibody titre, and in seronegative or immunocompromised hosts lacking endogenous antibody responses. Among RCTs, 4 of 6 found benefit with earlier administration, 3 of 4 with higher-titre CP, and two large RCTs (n=12,522) showed preferential benefit in seronegative recipients.
Controversies and gaps
Characterisation of non-IgG immunoglobulins (IgA/IgM) in CP products is nearly absent, with only 2 of 31 studies reporting such data despite evidence of their potent neutralising activity against SARS-CoV-2, representing a systematic gap in product assessment.
Research and practice implications
Future research should prioritise standardised quantification of IgA/IgM titres in CP products and evaluate precision deployment strategies for passive immunotherapy in future pandemics, leveraging timing and titre determinants to optimise efficacy.
